Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The radioactivity operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 821. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 330 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the radioactivity decomposition. By minimality, the element a^821 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 330-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
